\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}^{-1}(t)\mathcal{D}_{t}^{-1}\overline{M}\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{Q}\tilde{w}_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{Q}\tilde{w}_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{Q}(t)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{Q}^{-1}\left(t\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right).

Hence, we obtain the following equation for
\tilde{w}_{\pm}

i\partial_{t}\tilde{w}_{\pm}=\mathcal{Q}^{-1}\left(t\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{Q}\left(t\right)\tilde{w}_{\pm}\right).

(2.81)

The quantities
M
and
\mathcal{D}_{t}
are defined in (
1.9
) and
(
1.10
), and
\mathcal{Q}
is defined in (
A.128
) (see also
(
A.120
) and (
A.126
)). By (
A.128
) and (
A.138
), equation
(
2.81
) can equivalently be written as,

i\partial_{t}w_{\pm}=\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right),\qquad k\in\mathbb{R}^{+}.

(2.82)

For the definition of
\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)
see
(
A.137
). Moreover, by (
2.72
), (
A.18
) and (
A.138
),
equation (
2.82
) is also satisfied for
k\in\mathbb{R}^{-},
and
then, we have,

i\partial_{t}w_{\pm}=\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right),\qquad k\in{\mathbb{R}}.

(2.83)

Furthermore the solution
w_{\pm}
to (
2.83
) satisfies the symmetry,

S(k)w_{\pm}(t,-k)=w_{\pm}(t,k),\qquad|t|>0,k\in{\mathbb{R}}.

(2.84)

Moreover, integrating (
2.83
) from
a>0,
to
t>a,
and from
t<-a<0,
to

-a,
we obtain,

\begin{array}[c]{l}w_{\pm}(t)=w(\pm a)\pm\frac{1}{i}\,\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)\,d\tau.\end{array}

(2.85)

From (
2.85
) we see that
w_{\pm}
satisfies the symmetry (
2.84
)
for
t\geq a,
respectively
t\leq-a,
if and only if it satisfies it for

t=\pm a.
Summing up (see Lemma
A.11
), solving problem (
1.1
) with
a solution
u_{\pm}(t)\in H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\cap L_{2}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})

for
t\in[a,\infty),
respectively, for
t\in(-\infty,-a],

a>0,
is
equivalent to solving equation (
2.83
) with a solution
w_{\pm}(t)\in H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})\cap L_{2}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})
for
t\in[a,\infty),

respectively, for
t\in(-\infty,-a],a>0,
that satisfies the symmetry,

S(k)w_{\pm}(-k)=w(k),k\in{\mathbb{R}},
where
S(k),k\in{\mathbb{R}},
is the
scattering matrix for the linear matrix Schödinger equation defined in
(
A.17
). We find it convenient to consider (
2.85
) in
the full line, for the purpose of constructing global solutions to
(
1.1
), and in order to study their asymptotic behaviour for large times.

We recall that the quantities
\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,T),0\leq a<T\leq\infty,
were defined in (
1.11
). For
a>0,
and a measurable function

w(t)
of
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,T),
with values in
H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})

we define,

\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w\right)=\left\|w(t)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(\left\|w(t)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\langle t\right\rangle^{-1/2}\left\|w(t)\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.86)

The main estimates that we need are presented in the following lemma, which we
announce here but defer its proof to Section
4
below. We recall
that
P_{\pm}
are, respectively, the orthogonal projectors onto the
eigenvalues plus and minus one of the scattering matrix at zero energy,
S(0).
We recall that
\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)
was defined in (
2.85
).

Lemma 2.2

Suppose that the potential
V
is selfadjoint. Further,
assume that the boundary matrices
A,B
, satisfy (
1.5
), (
1.6
),
and that
H_{A,B,V}
does not have negative eigenvalues. Moreover, suppose
that the nonlinearity
\mathcal{N}
fulfills (
1.13
). Let

a,T
be positive numbers,
\infty\geq T>a>0.
Then, for some constant
C>0,

that is independent of
T,
and is uniform for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
the following
estimates are true. For any measurable function,
w_{\pm}(t),
of

t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T\right),
with values in
H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})
we have:

1.

Suppose that
V\in L^{1}_{2+\delta}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),\delta>0.
Then,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\\
+\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\\
\leq C\frac{|t\mp a|}{\langle t\rangle}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}(\tau)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.\end{array}

(2.87)

2.

If, moreover,
V\in L_{7/2+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for
some
\tilde{\delta}>0,

V
admits a regular decomposition with
\delta>3/2,

the nonlinearity
\mathcal{N}
commutes with the projector
P_{-}
onto the
eigenspace of the scattering matrix
S(0)
corresponding to the eigenvalue

-1,
i.e.

\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|\mu_{n}\right|\right)P_{-}=P_{-}\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\mu_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|\mu_{n}\right|\right),\text{ }\mu\in\mathbb{R}^{n},

w_{\pm}
satisfies (
2.83
), and
w_{\pm}(k)=S(k)w_{\pm}(-k),k\in{\mathbb{R}},
the following estimates hold,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\langle k\rangle\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t,w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,\end{array}

(2.88)

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\partial_{k}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t,w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,\end{array}

(2.89)

and, if in addition
V\in L^{1}_{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
and
V
admits a regular
decomposition with
\delta=2,
we have,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\langle{k}\rangle^{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right)\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,\end{array}

(2.90)

and

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(\tau^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}(\tau))_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right)\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.\end{array}

(2.91)

We now give the proof of Theorem
1.3

Proof of Theorem
1.3
.
By Theorem
2.1
we know that
problem (
1.1
) has unique solutions

u_{\pm}(t)\in C\left(\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(0,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right);H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\cap L_{2}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\right)\cap C^{1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(0,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right);L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\right),

and that if
\varepsilon
in (
1.14
) is small enough
T_{\pm,\text{max}}\geq T_{\varepsilon}>0.
Further, we know that
w_{\pm}(t)
defined in
(
2.72
) satisfies

w_{\pm}(t)\in C\left(\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right);H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\cap L_{2}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\right)\cap C^{1}\left(\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right);L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\right),\qquad T_{\pm,\text{max}}\geq T_{\varepsilon}>a>0.

Moreover,
w_{\pm}
is a solution to (
2.84
) and to (
2.85
), for

t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right)
, with

T_{\pm,\text{max}}\geq T_{\varepsilon}>a>0.

Using that,

\partial_{k}(k^{2}\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\overline{w})=k^{2}\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\partial_{k}\overline{w_{\pm}}+2k\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\overline{w_{\pm}}+k^{2}\partial_{k}^{2}w_{\pm}\overline{w_{\pm}},

and approximating
w_{\pm}
by functions in
C^{\infty}_{0}({\mathbb{R}})
in
the norm of
H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})\cap L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}}),
we obtain,

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}k^{2}\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\partial_{k}\overline{w_{\pm}}\,dk=-2\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}k\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\overline{w_{\pm}}dk+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}k^{2}\partial^{2}_{k}w_{\pm}\overline{w_{\pm}}\,dk.

Then, by the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\left\|k\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq 2\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|kw_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}_{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

and hence,

\left\|k\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left[\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right].

(2.92)

Note that by (
2.72
) and (
A.24
),

e^{-itk^{2}}w_{\pm}=\mathbf{F}u_{\pm}.

(2.93)

Then, by (
2.65
), (
2.66
), (
2.92
),
(
A.213
), and (
A.214
)

\|w_{\pm}(a)\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|w_{\pm}(a)\|_{L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon},\,\text{{with}}\,\tilde{\varepsilon}:=\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon^{\alpha}).

(2.94)

We will obtain an a priori bound for
w_{\pm}(t),

t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right).
For this purpose, we introduce the
following notation,

\Gamma_{\pm}(t):=\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left[\|w_{\pm}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\langle\tau\rangle}}\|w_{\pm}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right],\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.95)

We first observe that using (
2.85
), (
2.87
), and (
2.94
)
we have the estimate

\left\|w_{\pm}\left(t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\,\tilde{\varepsilon}+C\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.96)

Moreover, by (
2.85
), (
2.89
) and (
2.94
)

\left\|\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\left(t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}+C(1+\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha})\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(2.97)

and, using (
2.91
) instead of (
2.89
),

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}w_{\pm}\left(t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}+\sqrt{|t|}\,C(1+\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha})\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.98)

It follows from (
2.83
), (
2.85
), (
2.90
), and (
2.94
),

\left\|w_{\pm}\left(t\right)\right\|_{L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}+C\sqrt{|t|}(1+\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha})\Gamma_{\pm}(t)^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.99)

Let us designate,

\beta_{\pm}(t):=\Gamma_{\pm}(t)+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{1}{|\tau|^{1/2}}\left\|w_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\right\|_{L_{2}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.100)

Then, by (
2.96
), (
2.97
), (
2.98
) and (
2.99
)

\beta_{\pm}(t)\leq 6\,C\tilde{\varepsilon}+C\,\left(\beta^{\alpha}+1\right)\beta^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.101)

Take
\tilde{\varepsilon}
so small that,

C\left((7C\tilde{\varepsilon})^{\alpha}+1\right)(7C\tilde{\varepsilon})^{\alpha+1}<\frac{1}{2}C\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.102)

Suppose that for some
\theta_{\pm}
, with
a\leq\theta_{+}<T_{+,\mathrm{max}},
respectively,
T_{-,\mathrm{max}}<\theta_{-}\leq-a,

\beta_{\pm}(\theta_{\pm})=7C\tilde{\varepsilon}.

(2.103)

Then, by (
2.101
), (
2.102
), (
2.103
) we reach the
contradiction,

7C\tilde{\varepsilon}\leq\left(6+\frac{1}{2}\right)C\tilde{\varepsilon}.

Then for all
\theta_{\pm}
either
\beta_{\pm}(\theta_{\pm})<7C\tilde{\varepsilon}
or
\beta_{\pm}(\theta)>7C\tilde{\varepsilon}.
However, since

\beta_{\pm}(\pm a)\leq 6\,C\,\tilde{\varepsilon},
if for some
\theta_{\pm},\beta_{\pm}(\theta_{\pm})>7C\tilde{\varepsilon},
by continuity, for some
other
\theta_{1,\pm},

\beta_{\pm}(\theta_{1,\pm})=7C\tilde{\varepsilon},

and this is not possible. Hence, we have proved the following a priori bound,

\beta_{\pm}(t)<7C\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right).

(2.104)

Then, by (
2.93
), (
2.104
), and (
A.213
),

\left\|u_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right),

(2.105)

and using (
2.92
), and (
A.214
) instead of (
A.213
),
we get,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|u_{\pm}\right\|_{L_{2}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left\|e^{-itk^{2}}w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{2}\left[\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L_{2}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right]\\
\leq C|t|^{5/2}\,\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right).\end{array}

(2.106)

Hence, if
T_{\pm,\mathrm{max}}<\infty,

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T_{\pm,\text{max}}\right)}\left(\left\|u_{\pm}(t)\right\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\|u_{\pm}(t)\|_{L_{2}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right)\leq C<\infty.

However, this is in contradiction with Theorem
2.1
. In consequence,

T_{\pm,\mathrm{max}}=\pm\infty.

It only remains to prove (
1.15
), (
1.16
), and (
1.17
). Using
(
2.85
), (
2.104
), and (
3.8
), we show that

\left\|w_{\pm}\left(t\right)-w_{\pm}\left(s\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\left(t\right)-w_{\pm}\left(s\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\alpha+1}|s|^{-\left(\alpha/2-1\right)},\qquad|t|>|s|.

(2.107)

Thus,
w_{\pm}\left(t\right)
is a Cauchy sequence, and hence, there
exists a limit
v_{\pm}\in L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})\cap L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}),

v_{\pm}:=\lim_{t\to\pm\infty}w_{\pm}(t),

(2.108)

and

\|w_{\pm}(t)-v_{\pm}\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\|w_{\pm}(t)-v_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\alpha+1}|t|^{-\left(\alpha/2-1\right)}.

(2.109)

Moreover, by (
2.104
)

\|v_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq 7\,C\,\tilde{\varepsilon}.

(2.110)

We denote by
w_{\pm\infty}\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
the restriction to
\mathbb{R}^{+}
of
v_{\pm},
i.e.,

w_{\pm\infty}(k):=v_{\pm}(k),\qquad k\in\mathbb{R}^{+}.

(2.111)

Since
H
has no eigenvalues
P_{c}(H)=I.
Hence, by (
2.72
) and
(
A.130
) we get

u_{\pm}\left(t\right)=M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}.

(2.112)

Therefore, using (
2.104
), (
2.109
), (
A.253
), and
(
A.265
) we get,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|u_{\pm}\left(t\right)-M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)v_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\left\|\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\right)w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}+\left\|M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(w_{\pm}-v_{\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\\
\\
\ \leq C\left(\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\alpha+1}\,|t|^{-\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}-1\right)}+\tilde{\varepsilon}|t|^{-1/2}\right),\qquad|t|\geq a.\end{array}

(2.113)

Moreover, by (
2.104
), (
2.109
), (
2.112
), (
A.30
) and
(
A.266
),

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|u_{\pm}\left(t\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}M\mathcal{D}_{t}\left(m\left(\frac{x}{2},tx\right)w_{\pm\infty}\left(\frac{x}{2}\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq\left\|u_{\pm}\left(t\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}M\mathcal{D}_{t}\left(m\left(\frac{x}{2},tx\right)w_{\pm}\left(t,\frac{x}{2}\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\\
\\
+\left\|\ \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}M\mathcal{D}_{t}\left(m\left(\frac{x}{2},tx\right)(w_{\pm}\left(t,\frac{x}{2}\right)-w_{\pm\infty}\left(\frac{x}{2}\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C|t|^{-1/2}\left(\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\alpha+1}|t|^{-\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}-1\right)}+\tilde{\varepsilon}\,|t|^{-1/4}\right),|t|>a.\end{array}

(2.114)

Equation (
2.114
) proves (
1.16
). Observe that (
2.110
),
(
2.111
), (
2.114
), and (
A.30
) imply the
L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})
decay estimate (
1.17
). It follows from (
2.72
) and
(
2.108
), that

v_{\pm}(k)=S(k)v_{\pm}(-k).

(2.115)

Further, by (
A.237
) and(
A.240
)

\begin{array}[c]{l}M\mathcal{D}_{t}\mathcal{V}(t)v_{\pm}-e^{-itH_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{\dagger}w_{\pm\infty}=M\mathcal{D}_{t}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{-it(k-\frac{x}{2})^{2}}(S(k)-S_{0}(k))v_{\pm}(-k)dk\\
\\
=M\mathcal{D}_{t}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)dk.\end{array}

(2.116)

We have,

e^{-it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}=\frac{\partial_{k}\left(\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)e^{-it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)}{1-2it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}.

(2.117)

Using (
2.117
) and integrating by parts we obtain,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)dk=\displaystyle-\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\frac{x}{2}e^{-it\left(\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{1-2it\left(\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}(S(0)-S_{0}(0))v_{\pm}(0)\\
\\
-\sqrt{\frac{i|t|}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}4it\frac{\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1-2it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)dk\\
\\
-\sqrt{\frac{i|t|}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}\frac{k+\frac{x}{2}}{1-2it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\partial_{k}\left[(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)\right]dk.\end{array}

(2.118)

Note that,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left|\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}4it\frac{\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(1-2it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)dk\right|\\
\\
\leq C\int_{\sqrt{|t|}x/2}^{\infty}\frac{z^{2}}{\left(1+z^{2}\right)^{2}}v_{\pm}(k)dk\|v_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{|t|}x}\|v_{\pm}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}.\end{array}

Moreover,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left|\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}\frac{k+\frac{x}{2}}{1-2it\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\partial_{k}\left[(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)\right]dk\right|\\
\\
\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{t}x}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{+}}\left|\partial_{k}\left[(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)\right]\right|dk\leq\displaystyle C\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{|t|}x}\|v_{\pm}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}.\end{array}

Hence, by (
2.110
), (
A.18
), (
A.62
), and
Sobolev’s inequality,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-it(k+\frac{x}{2})^{2}}(S(-k)-S_{0}(-k))v_{\pm}(k)dk\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}\left[\sqrt{|t|}\left\|\frac{x}{1+tx^{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right.\\
\\
\left.+\left\|\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{|t|x}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right].\end{array}

(2.119)

Further, by (
2.113
), (
2.116
), and (
2.119
)

\left\|u_{\pm}\left(t\right)-e^{-itH_{0}}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{\dagger}w_{\pm\infty}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C\left(\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\alpha+1}\,|t|^{-\left(\frac{\alpha}{2}-1\right)}+\tilde{\varepsilon}|t|^{-1/4}\right),\qquad|t|\geq a.

(2.120)

This proves (
1.15
), and completes the proof of Theorem

1.3
.

Remark 2.3

By (
2.100
) and (
2.104
) with
T_{\pm,\text{max}}=\infty,

\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{|t|}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{|t|}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}_{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(2.121)

and by (
2.88
), and (
2.121
),

\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}_{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(2.122)

Further, by (
2.65
), (
2.66
),(
2.105
)
with
T_{\pm,\text{max}}=\infty,
we have,

\left\|u_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon}\langle\sqrt{|t|}\rangle,\qquad\pm t\geq 0.

(2.123)

Moreover, by (
2.65
), (
2.66
), (
2.93
),
(
2.122
), and (
A.212
),

\left\|u_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tilde{\varepsilon},\qquad\pm t\geq 0.

(2.124)

3
Auxiliary estimates.

In this section we prove the estimates that we need. We relay on the results
in Appendix
A
. Let
w_{\pm}
be functions from
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0,
with values in
H^{1}({\mathbb{R}}).
We denote

f_{1,\pm}:=\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm},

(3.1)

and

f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}:=\mathcal{N}\left(\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{1}\right|,\dots\left|(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm})_{n}\right|\right)\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}.

(3.2)

Lemma 3.1

Assume that the nonlinear function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy
(
1.13
). Let
w_{\pm}
be functions from
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0,
with values in
H^{1}({\mathbb{R}}).

Then, there is a constant
C
with the following properties.

1.

Suppose that the
V\in L_{2+\delta}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some

\delta>0.
Then

\left\|f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.3)

\left\|f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.4)

2.

\left\|f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq|t|^{-\alpha/2}C\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.5)

\left\|f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.6)

\left\|\partial_{x}^{j}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{j}({\mathbb{R}})},\qquad j=1,2,\pm t\geq a.

(3.7)

Here, for
j=2,
we assume that
w_{\pm}(t,\cdot)\in H^{2}({\mathbb{R}}).

Proof.
Equation (
3.3
) follows from (
A.140
), (
3.4
) follows from
(
A.152
). Equation (
3.5
) follows from (
A.253
), and
(
3.6
) follows from (
A.262
). Further, (
3.7
)
is a consequence of (
1.13
), (
A.259
), and
(
A.262
). Moreover, to prove each one of these equations we use
Sobolev’s inequality.

We also need the following:

Lemma 3.2

Suppose that
V\in L_{2+\delta}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some
\delta>0.
Let
the nonlinear function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Let

a,T
be positive numbers,
\infty\geq T>a>0.
Then, for some constant
C>0,

that is independent of
T,
and is uniform for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
the following
estimates are true. For any measurable function,
w_{\pm}(t),
of

t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,T\right),
with values in
H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})
we have

\left\|\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.8)

Proof.
Equation (
3.8
) follows from (
A.140
), (
A.152
) and Sobolev’s inequality.

Lemma 3.3

Suppose that
V\in L_{2+\delta}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some
\delta>0,

that the boundary matrices
A,B
, satisfy (
1.5
), (
1.6
), and
that
H_{A,B,V}
does not have negative eigenvalues. Let the nonlinear
function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Let
\zeta\in C^{2}\left(\mathbb{R}\right)
such that

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{j}\left(\zeta-1\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left\langle k\right\rangle^{-1},\text{ }j=0,1,

(3.9)

\displaystyle\text{\ }\left|\partial^{2}\left(\zeta-1\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C.

(3.10)

Then, for some constant
C>0,
that is independent of
T,
and is uniform for

a\geq a_{0}>0,
the following estimates are true. Assume that
w_{\pm}

satisfies (
2.83
) for
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0.
Then, the following is true:

\partial_{t}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)\right)=\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}(t)+\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}(t),

(3.11)

where

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-3/2}\left(|t|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right)\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.12)

and

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+|t|^{-1/2}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}(t)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-1}\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.13)

Proof.
To simplify the notation, in the calculations below we denote by
f_{1,\pm}
,
and
f^{\mathrm{fr}}_{1,\pm},
respectively, the restriction of
f_{1,\pm},

and of
f^{\mathrm{fr}}_{1,\pm},
to
k\geq 0.
Using that
w_{\pm}
satisfies
(
2.83
) and (
A.252
) we calculate

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{4t^{2}}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)+\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta\partial_{t}w_{\pm}

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{4t^{2}}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)+\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}\right).

(3.14)

First, by (
A.253
)

\left\|\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}.

(3.15)

Observe that by (
A.139
)

\widehat{\mathcal{W}}(t)f_{1,\pm}(k)=\mathcal{E}\left(\chi_{(0,\infty)}(k)\widehat{\mathcal{W}}(t)f_{1}(k)\right),\qquad k\in{\mathbb{R}}.

(3.16)

Next, since
H_{A,B,V}
does not have negative eigenvalues, it follows from
(
3.16
), (
A.128
) and (
A.138
),

\mathcal{W}(t)\widehat{\mathcal{W}}(t)f_{1,\pm}=\mathcal{W}(t)\mathcal{E}\left(\chi_{(0,\infty)}(k)\widehat{\mathcal{W}}(t)f_{1,\pm}(k)\right)=\mathcal{Q}(t)\mathcal{Q}^{-1}(t)f_{1,\pm}=f_{1,\pm}.

(3.17)

Then, we decompose

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\left(f_{1,\pm}-f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta-1\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1\pm,}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+f_{1,\pm}.

(3.18)

Using (
3.3
), (
A.140
), (
A.253
), and (
A.277
) with
n=0,
we
show

\left\|\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\right)f_{1,\pm,}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C_{a}|t|^{-1/2}\left\|f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\leq C_{a}\frac{1}{|t|^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.19)

By (
1.13
), (
A.152
) and (
A.275
) we get

\left\|f_{1,\pm}-f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C_{a}\frac{1}{|t|^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}}\left\|(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{V}\left(t\right))w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\leq C_{a}\frac{1}{|t|^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.20)

Further, it follows from (
A.140
), (
A.253
), (
A.258
), and
(
3.20
),

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\left(f_{1,\pm}-f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|f_{1,\pm}-f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}

\displaystyle\leq C_{a}\frac{1}{|t|^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.21)

From (
A.247
), integrating by parts, for
\phi\in H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),

and using,

\displaystyle\partial_{k}e^{it(k\pm x/2)^{2}}=\pm 2\partial_{x}e^{it(k\pm x/2)^{2}},

(3.22)

we have,

\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)\phi=\mp 2\sqrt{\frac{t}{2\pi i}}e^{itk^{2}}\phi\left(0\right)\mp 2\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)\left(\partial_{x}\phi\right).

(3.23)

Further, by (
3.5
), (
3.6
), (
3.7
),
(
3.9
), (
3.23
), (
A.18
), (
A.62
)
(
A.248
), (
A.258
), (
A.260
) and (
A.262
),

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta-1\right)\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sum_{\sigma=\pm}\left(\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+|t|^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2p_{1}}}\left\|\left\langle k\right\rangle^{-1}\right\|_{L^{q_{1}}}\left\|f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right.\\
\\
\left.+|t|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+|t|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\leq C_{a}|t|^{-\alpha/2}\,|t|^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2p_{1}}}\,\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.\end{array}

(3.24)

Moreover, (
3.5
), (
3.6
), (
3.7
), (
3.23
),
(
A.18
), (
A.62
) (
A.248
), (
A.257
),
(
A.260
), (
A.262
), and (
A.275
), we have,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\langle t\right\rangle^{1/2}\left\|\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sum_{\sigma=\pm}\left(\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+|t|^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2p_{1}}}\left\|\left\langle k\right\rangle^{-1}\right\|_{L^{q_{1}}}\left\|f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\right.\\
\\
\left.+|t|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+|t|^{-\frac{1}{4}}\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\sigma}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\\
\\
\leq C_{a}|t|^{-\alpha/2}\,|t|^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2p_{1}}}\,\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.\end{array}

(3.25)

By (
3.4
), (
3.7
), (
3.23
), and (
A.260
), we
get

\left\|\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C_{a}\sqrt{|t|}\,|t|^{-\alpha/2}\,\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.26)

Then, using (
3.3
), (
3.9
), (
3.26
), (
A.253
), and
(
A.257
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta-1\right)\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+C\left\|\left(\zeta-1\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C_{a}\sqrt{|t|}\,|t|^{-\alpha/2}\,\Lambda_{\pm}(t;w_{\pm}),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.27)

We denote (see (
2.27
)),

\begin{array}[c]{l}\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}:=\frac{i}{4t^{2}}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\zeta w_{\pm}\right)+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(t\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)\\
+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\zeta-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)\left(f_{1,\pm}-f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)+\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1\pm,}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),\\
\end{array}

\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}:=\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\left(\zeta-1\right)\right)\left(\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(t\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+f_{1,\pm}.

(3.28)

Therefore, since
\alpha>2,
and using (
3.14
), (
3.18
), (
3.15
)
(
3.19
), (
3.21
), (
3.24
), (
3.25
), (
3.27
), and taking

p_{1}>1
sufficiently close to
1,
we prove (
3.11
), (
3.12
), and (
3.13
).

Lemma 3.4

Let the nonlinear function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Let

w_{\pm}
be functions from
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0,
with values in
H^{2}({\mathbb{R}}).
Then, the following is true:

1.

\left\|\partial_{t}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{-\alpha/2}\left[\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\partial_{t}w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\right],\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.29)

2.

Suppose that
V\in L_{2+\delta}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some

\delta>0,
and that
H_{A,B,V}
does not have negative eigenvalues. Assume
that
w_{\pm}
satisfies (
2.83
) for
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0.
Then, we have

\partial_{t}f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}=\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}+\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{\left(1\right)},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.30)

where

\left\|\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}|t|^{-\alpha/2}\,|t|^{-3/2}\left(|t|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right)\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.31)

and,

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}|t|^{-\alpha/2}|t|^{-1}\left(|t|^{-1/2}\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right.+\\
\left.\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right)\right)+C|t|^{-1-\alpha/2}\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{(1)}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.\\
\\
\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{(1)}(t)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}|t|^{-\alpha/2}|t|^{-1/2}\left(|t|^{-1/2}\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}\right.+\\
\left.\Lambda_{\pm}\left(t;w_{\pm}\right)\right)+C|t|^{-1-\alpha/2}\|w_{\pm}\|_{H^{(1)}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\ \geq a.\end{array}

(3.32)

3.

Suppose that
V\in L_{3+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some

\tilde{\delta}>1/2,
and that it admits a regular decomposition (see
Definition
1.1
) with
\delta>0,
that
w_{\pm}
satisfies (
2.83
)
for
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0.
Then, we have,

\partial_{t}f_{1,\pm}=\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}^{\left(2\right)}+\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{\left(2\right)},

(3.33)

where,

(3.34)

(3.35)

\\
+\mathcal{N}\left(t^{-1/2}\left|\left(\mathcal{V}(t)w_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,\dots,\left|t^{-1/2}\left(\mathcal{V}(t)w_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\,\mathcal{A}_{l,\pm}-\\

Lemma 3.5

Suppose that
V\in L_{3+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some

\tilde{\delta}>n+1/2.

(

\left\|x^{n}\partial_{t}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{1/2-n-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\partial_{t}w\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.45)

2.

V\in L^{1}_{4+\tilde{\delta}}({\mathbb{R}}),

) for

(3.46)

Proof.
Using (
2.27
) with

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f_{2,\pm}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|t|^{1/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left[\left(E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\partial_{t}\left(\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}-\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left(E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}-E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\partial_{t}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}

\displaystyle+\left(E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\partial_{t}\left(\overline{\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}-\overline{\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}\right)

(3.47)

\displaystyle\left.+\left(E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}-E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\partial_{t}\overline{\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathcal{N}\left(\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,...,\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\left(\partial_{t}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{N}\left(\left|t^{-1/2}\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|...,\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right.

\displaystyle-\mathcal{N}\left.\left(\left|\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{1}\right|,...,\left|t^{-1/2}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{n}\right|\right)\partial_{t}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\right)w_{\pm}.\pm

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|t|^{3/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left[\left(E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}-\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left(E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}-E_{1}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}

\displaystyle+\left(E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(\overline{\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}-\overline{\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle\left.+\left(E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}-E_{2}^{\left(j\right)}\left(t^{-1/2}\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)\overline{\left(\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)w_{\pm}\right)_{j}}\right].

(3.51)

for
\pm t\geq a.

Lemma 3.6

Suppose that
V\in L_{2+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some

\tilde{\delta}\geq n,
if
n>0,
and
\tilde{\delta}>0,
if
n=0.
Further,
assume that
H_{A,B,V}
does not have negative eigenvalues. In addition,
suppose that
V
admits a regular decomposition (see Definition
1.1
)
with
\delta=\min[n-1,0].
Let the nonlinear function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy
(
1.13
). Let
w_{\pm}
be functions from
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0,
with values in
H^{1}({\mathbb{R}}).

Then, the inequality

\left\|x^{n}\partial_{x}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{1-n-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.61)

is true. In addition, if
V
admits a regular decomposition for some

\delta\geq n-1+1/2,
if
n>0,
and
\delta=0,
for
n=0,
the estimate

\left\|x^{n}\partial_{x}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|t|^{1/2-n-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(3.62)

holds.

Proof.
We first prepare an estimate that we need for the proof of this lemma, and
also for later purposes. Since,

e^{-it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}=\frac{\partial_{k}\left(\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)e^{-it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)}{1-2it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}},

(3.63)

integrating by parts we have

\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}e^{-it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}g\left(k\right)dk=-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{2it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}e^{it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}}{\left(1-2it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}g\left(k\right)dk-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{\left(\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)e^{it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)}{1-2it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\partial_{x}g\left(k\right)dk.

Then, by Hölder’s and Sobolev’s inequalities we get

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}e^{-it\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}g\left(k\right)dk\right|\leq C\left\|\frac{1}{1+|t|k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+C\left\|\frac{x}{1+|t|k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|g_{1}\right\|_{L^{q}({\mathbb{R}})}\\
+C\left\|\frac{k}{1+|t|k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{p_{1}}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|g_{2}\right\|_{L^{q_{2}}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{|t|}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{C}{|t|^{\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{p}\right)}}\left\|g_{1}\right\|_{L^{q}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{C}{|t|^{\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{p_{1}}\right)}}\left\|g_{2}\right\|_{L^{q_{1}}({\mathbb{R}})},\end{array}

(3.64)

for
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=\frac{1}{p_{1}}+\frac{1}{q_{1}}=1
and

p,p_{1}>1,
where
\partial_{x}g=g_{1}+g_{2}.
In order to prove
(
3.61
), we use (
3.47
) with
\partial_{t}
replaced by

\partial_{x}.
Let us first estimate
x^{n}\partial_{x}\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\right)w.
By (
A.126
),
(
A.146
), (
A.238
) and integrating by parts,

\partial_{x}\left(\mathcal{W}\left(t\right)-\mathcal{V}\left(t\right)\right)w_{\pm}=\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-it\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\left[\partial_{k}\left((m\left(k,tx\right)-I)w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right)+t\partial_{x}m(k,tx)w_{\pm}(k)\right]dk.

(3.65)

By (
A.31
) and (
A.32
),

\left\|\frac{1}{2}x^{n}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}e^{-it\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\partial_{k}\left((m\left(k,tx\right)-I)w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right)dk\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\left(t^{1/2-n}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.66)

Using (
3.64
) with
g=x^{n}\partial_{x}m(k,tx)w_{\pm}(k),

g_{1}=g_{2}=\partial_{k}x^{n}\partial_{x}m(k,tx),
and
p=q=2,
(
A.32
), and
(
A.33
), we get,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}e^{-it\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}t\partial_{x}m(k,tx)w_{\pm}(k)dk\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|tx^{n}\left\langle tx\right\rangle^{-1-\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(|t|^{1-n}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.67)

Then, estimate (
3.61
) follows from (
1.13
),
(
3.47
) with
\partial_{t},
replaced by
\partial_{x},
(
3.65
),
(
3.66
), (
3.67
) (
A.140
), (
A.152
), (
A.253
), and
(
A.262
).

Further, if
V
admits a regular decomposition for some
\delta\geq n-1+1/2,
if
n>0,
and
\delta=0,
for
n=0,
using (
3.64
) with
g=x^{n}\partial_{x}m(k,tx)w_{\pm}(k),

g_{1}=g_{2}=\partial_{k}x^{n}\partial_{x}m(k,tx),
and
p=q=2,
(
A.32
), and (
A.33
), we obtain,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{it}{2\pi}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}e^{-it\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}t\partial_{x}m(k,tx)w_{\pm}(k)dk\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|tx^{n}\left\langle tx\right\rangle^{-1-\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(|t|^{1/2-n}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(3.68)

Finally, estimate (
3.62
) follows from (
1.13
),
(
3.47
) with
\partial_{t},
replaced by
\partial_{x},
(
3.65
),
(
3.66
), (
3.68
) (
A.140
), (
A.152
), (
A.253
), and
(

).

Note that,

it(k\pm x/2)\,e^{it(k\pm x/2)^{2}}=\pm\partial_{x}e^{it(k\pm x/2)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\partial_{k}e^{it(k\pm x/2)^{2}}.

(4.2)

\displaystyle D_{1,\pm}=-\left(i\tau\right)^{-1}\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(m^{\dagger}\left(-k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(i\tau\right)^{-1}S\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(\partial_{k}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(-k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle-\left(i\tau\right)^{-1}\left(\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(\left(\partial_{k}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)

(4.3)

\displaystyle-S\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\left(m^{\dagger}\left(-k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)\right)+\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(xm^{\dagger}\left(k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\right).

\left\|D_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\left\|\langle x\rangle f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\,\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{L_{1}^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right),\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.4)

Then, (

), (
4.4

).

).
By (

)

\displaystyle k^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[k\partial_{k}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}+kD_{1,\pm}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[\partial_{k}\left(k\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}+kD_{1,\pm}\right]

(4.5)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[\frac{1}{2}\partial_{k}^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{k}D_{1,\pm}-\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}+kD_{1,\pm}\right].

(4.6)

and using also (

), we get,

(4.7)

Lemma 4.1

Suppose that
V\in L_{5+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}({\mathbb{R}}),

\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\partial_{k}^{2}\theta_{\pm}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

Proof.

4.3
.

(4.13)

for

4.3
Estimates for the derivatives of
\theta_{\pm}.
Proof of Lemma

4.1

This Section is devoted to the proof of Lemma
4.1
. In order to prove
this result, we prepare several estimates. Let us denote

\theta_{\pm}^{(1)}:=\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}\right)-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),\theta_{\pm}^{(2)}:=\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),

(4.14)

and

\tilde{\theta}_{\pm}:=\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{2}f_{1,\pm}\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{2}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

(4.15)

We define

R_{1}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right):=\mp\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\left(\partial_{k}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(\mp k,\tau x\right)\phi\left(x\right)dx,

(4.16)

and

R_{2}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right):=R_{21}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right)+R_{22}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right),

(4.17)

with

R_{21}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right):=\mp 4i\tau\left(\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\left(\partial_{k}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(\mp k,\tau x\right)\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)\phi\left(x\right)dx\right),

(4.18)

and

R_{22}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right):=\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\left(\partial_{k}^{2}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(\mp k,\tau x\right)\phi\left(x\right)dx.

(4.19)

Also, we set

\Phi_{1,\pm}:=|\tau|^{1+\alpha/2}\left\|\theta_{\pm}^{(1)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+|\tau|^{1+\alpha/2}\left\|\theta_{\pm}^{(2)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

(4.20)

\begin{array}[c]{l}\Phi_{2,\pm}:=|\tau|^{\alpha/2}\left[\left\|R_{1}^{+}\left(f_{1,\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|R_{1}^{-}\left(f_{1,\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\right.\\
\left.\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}{{}_{+}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right],\end{array}

(4.21)

\Phi_{3,\pm}:=|\tau|^{\alpha/2}|\left\|\tau\theta_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\tau^{2}\tilde{\theta}_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

(4.22)

\begin{array}[c]{l}\Phi_{4,\pm}:=|\tau|^{\alpha/2}\left[\left\|R_{2}^{+}\left(f_{1,\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}{\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|R_{2}^{-}\left(f_{1,\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right],\end{array}

(4.23)

\Phi_{5,\pm}:=|\tau|^{\alpha/2}\left[\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right],

(4.24)

\Phi_{6,\pm}:=|\tau|^{\alpha/2}\left[\left\|\left(\partial_{k}^{2}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\left(\partial_{k}^{2}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right],

(4.25)

\Omega_{1,\pm}:=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau k\theta_{\pm}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},\Omega_{2,\pm}:=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{2}k\theta_{\pm}^{(1)}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{2}k\theta_{\pm}^{(2)}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

(4.26)

and

\Omega_{3,\pm}:=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{2}k^{2}\theta_{\pm}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}.

(4.27)

We begin with the following:

Lemma 4.4

Suppose that
V\in L_{4}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}).
Let the nonlinear function

\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Then, for some
C>0,
uniformly
for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
and for any measurable function,
w_{\pm}(t),
of

t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),
with values in

H^{1}({\mathbb{R}}),
we have

\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\partial_{k}\theta_{\pm}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left(\Omega_{1,\pm}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\Phi_{1,\pm}+\Phi_{2,\pm}\right)\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(4.28)

and

\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\partial_{k}^{2}\theta_{\pm}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left(\Omega_{2,\pm}+\Omega_{3,\pm}+\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\sum_{j=3}^{6}\Phi_{j\pm,}\right)\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.29)

Proof.
We calculate

\partial_{k}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi=2i\tau k\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi\pm i\tau\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)+R_{1}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right),

and

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle=2i\tau\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi-4\tau^{2}k^{2}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle\mp 4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)-\tau^{2}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{2}\phi\left(x\right)\right)+R_{2}^{\pm}\left(\phi\right)+2i\tau kR^{\pm}_{1}(\phi)\pm i\tau R^{\pm}_{1}(x\phi).

Then,

\displaystyle\partial_{k}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle=2i\tau k\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-i\tau\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)+i\tau\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(xS\left(k\right)\phi\left(x\right)\right)

(4.30)

\displaystyle+R_{1}^{-}\left(\phi\right)+R_{1}^{+}\left(S\left(k\right)\phi\right)+\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\phi,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle=2i\tau\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-4\tau^{2}k^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-\tau^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{2}\phi\left(x\right)\right)

\displaystyle+4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)-4\tau^{2}kS\left(k\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)

\displaystyle+R_{2}^{-}\left(\phi\right)+R_{2}^{+}\left(S\left(k\right)\phi\right)

(4.31)

\displaystyle+2\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\phi+\partial_{k}^{2}S\left(k\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\phi+2i\tau k\left[R^{-}_{1}(\phi)+S(k)R^{+}_{1}(\phi)\right]+

\displaystyle i\tau\left[-R^{-}_{1}(x\phi)+S(k)R^{+}_{1}(x\phi)\right].

Using the above relations replacing the function
m
by
1,
we get

\displaystyle\partial_{k}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle=2i\tau k\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-i\tau\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)

\displaystyle+i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(xS\left(k\right)\phi\left(x\right)\right)+\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\phi,

(4.32)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\phi

\displaystyle=2i\tau\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-4\tau^{2}k^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\phi-\tau^{2}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{2}\phi\left(x\right)\right)

\displaystyle+4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)-4\tau^{2}kS\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\phi\left(x\right)\right)

(4.33)

\displaystyle+2\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\phi+\partial_{k}^{2}S\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\phi.

Then, (
4.28
) and (
4.29
) follow from (
4.14
)-(
4.3
),
(
A.62
) and (
A.63
).

Lemma 4.5

Suppose that
V\in L_{4}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
and that it admits
a regular decomposition with
\delta>0
(see Definition
1.1
). Let the
nonlinear function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Then, for
some
C>0,
uniformly for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
and for any measurable function,

w_{\pm}(t),
of
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),
with
values in
H^{1}({\mathbb{R}}),
we have

\sqrt{|\tau|}\Phi_{1,\pm}(\tau)+\Phi_{2,\pm}(\tau)\leq C\left\|w_{\pm}(\tau)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.34)

and

\left(\sum_{j=3}^{5}\Phi_{j,\pm}(\tau)\right)+\sqrt{|\tau|}\Phi_{6,\pm}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|w_{\pm}(\tau)\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.35)

Proof.
We decompose

\theta_{\pm}^{(j)}=\theta^{(j)}_{\pm,1}+\theta_{\pm,2}^{(j)},\qquad j=1,2,

(4.36)

where

\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(1\right)}:=\left(\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}\right),\qquad\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(2\right)}:=\left(\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(xf_{1,\pm}\right),

(4.37)

and

\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(1\right)}=\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{2,\pm}\right),\qquad\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(2\right)}=\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(xf_{2,\pm}\right).

(4.38)

Using (
3.3
) and (
A.276
) with
n=1
we have

\left\|\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(j\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3/2}\left\|f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad j=1,2,\pm\tau\geq a.

Since

\left|f_{2,\pm}\right|\leq C\left|\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}-\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right|\left(\left|\mathcal{W}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right|^{\alpha}\mathcal{+}\left|\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right|^{\alpha}\right),

(4.39)

by (
A.152
) and (
A.275
) with
\delta>n+1/2
we get

\left\|x^{n}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left\|x^{n}\left\langle\tau x\right\rangle^{-\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}\leq C|\tau|^{-1/2-n-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}.

(4.40)

Then, putting
n=1
and using (
A.258
) we show that

\left\|\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(j\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad j=1,2,\pm t\geq a.

Therefore

\left\|\theta_{\pm}^{(j)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}(\mathbb{R}^{+})})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad j=1,2,\pm t\geq a,

(4.41)

and hence,

\Phi_{1,\pm}\leq C|\tau|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.42)

Using (
A.31
) with
\delta>1/2
and (
3.3
) we get

\left\|R_{1}^{\pm}f_{1,+}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{-1}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|\left\langle\tau x\right\rangle^{-\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|f_{1,+}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{+}\right\|_{H^{1}}^{\alpha+1},

and

\left\|R_{1}^{\pm}f_{1,-}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|\left\langle\cdot\right\rangle^{-1}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|\left\langle\tau x\right\rangle^{-\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|f_{1,-}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{-}\right\|_{H^{1}}^{\alpha+1}.

Moreover, by (
3.3
), (
A.62
), (
A.141
),and
(
A.257
) we show

\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}.

Hence,

\Phi_{2,\pm}\leq C\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.43)

Using (
4.42
) and (
4.43
) we prove (
4.34
).

Writing

\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{n}f_{1,\pm}\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{n}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(x^{n}f_{1,\pm}\right)+\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{n}f_{2,\pm}\right),

and using (

), (

), (
A.277
), and (

\left\|\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{n}f_{1,\pm}\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(x^{n}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-1/2-n-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}.

(4.44)

Using this last equation with
n=0,
we estimate

\left\|\theta_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-1/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},

Using (
4.1
) and integrating by parts, we get

\displaystyle\left.\left\|\left\langle\tau x\right\rangle^{-\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\left\|\partial_{x}f_{1,+}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)

Lemma 4.6

Suppose that
V\in L_{5+\tilde{\delta}}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some
\tilde{\delta}>0
and that
H_{A,B,V}
does not
have negative eigenvalues. In addition, assume that
V
admits a regular
decomposition with
\delta>1
(see Definition
1.1
). Let the nonlinear
function
\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Suppose that
w_{\pm}

satisfies (
2.83
) for
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),

a>0.
Then, for some
C>0,
uniformly for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
we have,

\Omega_{1,\pm}+\Omega_{2,\pm}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a,

(4.52)

and

\Omega_{3,\pm}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right).\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.53)

Proof.
Define

\Theta^{\left(1\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right):=\tau k\theta_{\pm},

\Theta^{\left(2\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right):=\tau^{2}k\theta_{\pm}^{(1)},

\Theta^{\left(3\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right):=\tau^{2}k\theta_{\pm}^{(2)},

and

\Theta^{\left(4\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right):=\tau^{2}k^{2}\theta_{\pm}.

Then

\Omega_{1,\pm}=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\Theta^{\left(1\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})},

\Omega_{2,\pm}=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\Theta^{\left(2\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\Theta^{\left(3\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

and

\Omega_{3,\pm}=\left\|\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\Theta^{\left(4\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}.

We have,

1=e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\tau}\left(\tau e^{i\tau k^{2}}\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}.

(4.54)

Using (
4.54
) and integrating by parts, we have

\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau=\Theta_{1,\pm}^{\left(j\right)}+\Theta_{2,\pm}^{\left(j\right)}+\Theta_{3,\pm}^{\left(j\right)},

(4.55)

where

\Theta_{1,\pm}^{\left(j\right)}=\pm\vskip 12.0pt plus 4.0pt minus 4.0pt\left.\tau\frac{1}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)\right|_{\pm a}^{t},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

\Theta_{2,\pm}^{\left(j\right)}=\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{i\tau k^{2}}{\left(1+i\tau k^{2}\right)^{2}}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)d\tau,\qquad\pm t\geq a,

and

\Theta_{3,\pm}^{\left(j\right)}=-\int_{1}^{t}\tau\frac{e^{i\tau k^{2}}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)\right)d\tau,\qquad\pm t\geq a.

for
j=1,2,3.
Using (
4.41
) and (
4.45
), we obtain

(4.56)

for
j=1,2,3
and

\left\|\frac{1}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\Theta^{\left(4\right)}\left(\tau,k\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C|\tau|^{1/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.57)

Next, we estimate
\partial_{\tau}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right).
Let us control first
\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm}\right).
We decompose
\theta_{\pm}
as

\theta_{\pm}=\theta_{\pm}^{\left(a\right)}+\theta_{\pm}^{\left(b\right)},

(4.58)

with

\theta_{\pm}^{\left(a\right)}=\left(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}\left(\tau\right)-\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}

and

\theta^{\left(b\right)}_{\pm}=\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)f_{2,\pm}.

Observing that

\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)=\pm\frac{1}{\tau}e^{-i\tau k^{2}}x\partial_{x}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}-ie^{-i\tau k^{2}}\frac{x^{2}}{4}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}},

we calculate

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)f\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left[\frac{1}{2\tau}\mp\frac{2}{\tau}\right]f\mp\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f)\right)

\displaystyle\mp\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(\mp k,\tau x\right)f\left(x\right)dx,

(4.59)

and

e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)f\right)=\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left[\frac{1}{2\tau}\mp\frac{2}{\tau}\right]f\mp\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f\right).

(4.60)

Then, using (
4.59
) and (
4.60
) with
f=f_{1,\pm},
and (
A.137
),
(

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta^{\left(a\right)}_{\pm}\right)

\displaystyle=S(k)\left(\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(-\frac{3}{2\tau}f_{1,\pm}-\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle-S(k)\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(-k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)dx

\displaystyle+\left(\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(\frac{5}{2\tau}f_{1,\pm}+\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)dx.

Hence, using (

(4.61)

\begin{array}[c]{l}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta^{\left(b\right)}_{\pm}\right)=S(k)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(-\frac{3}{2\tau}f_{2,\pm}-\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f_{2,\pm}\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f_{2,\pm}\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f_{2,\pm}\right)\\
+\ \mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\frac{5}{2\tau}f_{2,\pm}+\frac{1}{\tau}x\partial_{x}f_{2,\pm}\left(x\right)-\frac{i}{4}x^{2}f_{2,\pm}\left(x\right)+\partial_{\tau}f_{2,\pm}\right).\end{array}

Then, using (
A.257

) with

(4.62)

Therefore, it follows from (

\left\|e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3/2-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.63)

Next, we control
\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm}^{(j)}\right),j=1,2.
Observing that

\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)=i\left(\pm kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)e^{-i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}},

we show

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)f\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{W}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\left(\frac{1}{2\tau}f+i\left(\pm kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)f\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k\pm\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(\mp k,\tau x\right)f\left(x\right)dx,

and

Then, with
\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(j\right)},
and
\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(j\right)},j=1,2
given respectively by (
4.37
) and (

)

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(1\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{W}_{+}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{2\tau}xf_{1,\pm}+i\left(kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)xf_{1,\pm}+x\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(-k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)dx,

(4.64)

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(2\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathcal{W}_{-}\left(\tau\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{2\tau}xf_{1,\pm}+i\left(-kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)xf_{1,\pm}+x\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{i\tau\left(k-\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}x\left(\partial_{x}m^{\dagger}\right)\left(k,\tau x\right)f_{1,\pm}\left(x\right)dx,

(4.65)

e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(1\right)}\right)=\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\frac{1}{2\tau}xf_{2,\pm}+i\left(kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)xf_{2,\pm}+x\partial_{\tau}f_{2,\pm}\right),

(4.66)

and

e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(2\right)}\right)=\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\frac{1}{2\tau}xf_{2,\pm}+i\left(-kx+\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)xf_{2,\pm}+x\partial_{\tau}f_{2,\pm}\right).

(4.67)

Using (
3.33
), (
A.32
), and (
A.276
) in (
4.64
),
(
4.65
) we show

\left\|\frac{k}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,1}^{\left(j\right)}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),

(4.68)

for
j=1,2
,
\pm\tau\geq a.
Since by (
A.247
),

\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|\phi\right\|_{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{+})},

using (
A.258
) in (
4.66
), (
4.67
) we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{k}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(j\right)}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{|\tau|^{3/2}}\left\|xf_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{C}{|\tau|}\left\|x^{2}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{|\tau|}}\left\|x^{3}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+C\left\|x\partial_{t}f_{2,\pm}\right\|_{L^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}.

Then, by (
3.46
) with
n=1
and (
4.40
) we derive

\left\|\frac{k}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm,2}^{\left(j\right)}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),j=1,2,\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.69)

Hence, using (
4.68
) and (
4.69
) in (
4.36
) we obtain

\left\|\frac{k}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\theta_{\pm}^{(j)}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-3-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),

(4.70)

for
j=1,2,\pm\tau\geq a.
Using (
4.41
), (
4.45
), (
4.63
) and
(
4.70
), we prove

\left\|\frac{e^{i\tau k^{2}}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\Theta^{\left(j\right)}_{\pm}\left(\tau,k\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-1-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),

(4.71)

for
\pm\tau\geq a,
for
j=1,2,3
and

\left\|\frac{e^{i\tau k^{2}}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\Theta^{\left(4\right)}\left(\tau,k\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\tau^{-1/2-\alpha/2}\left(\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda\left(\tau;w\right).

(4.72)

Therefore, it follows from (

), (

), (

) and
(

)

\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\Theta_{i}^{\left(j\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq Ct^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}+1}\left(\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda\left(t;w\right),

for
j=1,23,
and

\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\Theta_{i}^{\left(4\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq Ct^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}+3/2}\left(\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda\left(t;w\right).

Hence, from (
4.55
) we attain (
4.52
) and (
4.53
).

Proof of Lemma
4.1
.
