4.4
Estimates for the derivatives of
\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}.
Proof of Lemmas

4.2
and
4.3

Let
\chi\in C^{\infty}_{0}\left(\mathbb{R}\right)
be such that
\chi\geq 0,

\left\{\begin{array}[c]{c}\chi\left(x\right)=1,\text{ for\ \ }\left|x\right|\leq 1,\\
\chi\left(x\right)=0,\text{ for\ }\left|x\right|\geq 2.\end{array}\right.

(4.73)

In order to estimate the derivatives
\partial_{k}^{j}\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}},

j=1,2,
we prepare the
following lemma.

Lemma 4.7

Suppose that
V\in L^{1,3+\tilde{\delta}}(\mathbb{R}^{+}),
for some
\tilde{\delta}>1/2
and that
H_{A,B,V}
does not have
negative eigenvalues. In addition, suppose that
V
admits a regular
decomposition (see Definition
1.1
). Let the nonlinear function

\mathcal{N}
satisfy (
1.13
). Let
\psi\in C^{2}\left(\mathbb{R}\right)
be such that

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left|\psi\left(k\right)\right|\leq Ck\left\langle k\right\rangle^{-1}\text{, \ }\left|\psi^{\prime}\left(k\right)\right|\leq C\left\langle k\right\rangle^{-1},\\
\left|\psi^{\prime\prime}\left(k\right)\right|\leq C.\end{array}

(4.74)

Then, if
w_{\pm}
satisfies (
2.83
) for
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}\left(a,\infty\right),
there is a constant
C>0
, such that uniformly for
a\geq a_{0}>0,
we have,

\left\|\partial_{k}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\psi\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\pm t\geq a,

(4.75)

and,

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\psi\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\ \sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),

(4.76)

for
\pm t\geq a.

Proof.
Using (
4.54
) and integrating by parts we have

\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\psi\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau=A^{\left(1\right)}+A^{\left(2\right)}+\tilde{A},

(4.77)

where

A^{\left(1\right)}=\pm\left.\tau\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right|_{\pm a}^{t},

A^{\left(2\right)}=\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{i\tau k^{2}\psi\left(k\right)}{\left(1+i\tau k^{2}\right)^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau,

and

\tilde{A}=-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau.

Using (
4.1
) and integrating by parts we get

i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\phi=-\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\phi\left(0\right)-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\phi\right).

(4.78)

Then, using that

\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\right)=i\left(\frac{1}{2}x\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2}kx\right)e^{-i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}

we obtain,

\displaystyle e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{2}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)x\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}+\frac{i}{2}k\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)x\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}+\frac{1}{2\tau}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\tau}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(x\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+\frac{i}{2}k\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}+\frac{1}{2\tau}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1}^{\operatorname*{fr}}.

Thus, we decompose
\tilde{A}
as follows

\tilde{A}=A^{\left(3\right)}+A^{\left(4\right)}+A^{\left(5\right)}+A^{\left(6\right)},

where

A^{\left(3\right)}=\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(x\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau,

A^{\left(4\right)}=-\frac{i}{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)x\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau,

A^{\left(5\right)}=-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau,

and

A^{\left(6\right)}=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau.

Observe that

\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|\left\langle k\right\rangle\frac{k^{m}\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\left\langle k\right\rangle\partial_{k}\frac{k^{m}\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|k\partial_{k}\frac{k^{m}\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\frac{m}{2}},

(4.79)

for
m=0,1.
Then, by (
3.5
), (
3.7
), (
3.23
), and
(
A.257
) we show that

\left\|\partial_{k}A^{\left(j\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm t\geq a,

for
j=1,2,3,6.
Next, using (
3.23
), and (
4.78
) we decompose

\partial_{k}A^{\left(4\right)}=A^{\left(41\right)}+A^{\left(42\right)}+A^{\left(43\right)}+A^{\left(44\right)},

where

A^{\left(41\right)}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\pi i}}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\sqrt{\tau}\left(\partial_{k}\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\left(f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\left(0\right)d\tau,

A^{\left(42\right)}=\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\partial_{k}\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau,

A^{\left(43\right)}=i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\left(k\partial_{k}\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau,

and

A^{\left(44\right)}=i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau.

Thus, by (
3.5
), (
3.6
), (
3.7
), (
4.79
),
and (
A.257
) we prove

\left\|\partial_{k}A^{\left(4\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\pm t\geq a.

Next, using (
3.30
), as well as (
3.23
) we decompose

\displaystyle\partial_{k}A^{\left(5\right)}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\left(\partial_{k}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle-2i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\left(\chi\mathcal{A}_{1,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi i}}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{3/2}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)e^{i\tau k^{2}}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}\left(0\right)d\tau

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\chi\mathcal{A}_{2,\pm}^{\left(1\right)}\right)d\tau.

Hence, by (
3.31
), (
3.32
), (
4.79
) and (
A.257
), we get

\left\|\partial_{k}A^{\left(5\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}(){\mathbb{R}}}^{\alpha}\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right)\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

Therefore, using these estimates for
A^{\left(j\right)},

j=1,...,6,
in
(
4.77
) we obtain (
4.75
).

Now, we estimate (
4.76
). Using (
3.23
) we have

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\varphi\right)

\displaystyle=2i\tau\partial_{k}\left(\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\varphi\right)

\displaystyle=-4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{itk^{2}}\varphi\left(0\right)+2i\tau\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\varphi

\displaystyle-4i\tau\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\varphi\right)\right).

(4.80)

Then, it follows from (

), (

), (

),

), (

), (
4.80
), and (

) that

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\left(\frac{i\tau k^{2}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)^{m}\mathcal{V}_{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

for
m=0,1.
Hence, we show that

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}A^{\left(j\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.81)

for
j=1,2.
We decompose

\partial_{k}^{2}\tilde{A}=\tilde{A}^{\left(1\right)}+\tilde{A}^{\left(2\right)}+\tilde{A}^{\left(3\right)},

(4.82)

where

\tilde{A}^{\left(1\right)}=-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\left[\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau,

\tilde{A}^{\left(2\right)}=-2\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\partial_{k}\left(e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau,

and

\tilde{A}^{\left(3\right)}=-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{k}^{2}\left(e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau.

First, we estimate
\tilde{A}^{\left(1\right)}.
Integrating by parts in

\tau
we have,

\displaystyle\tilde{A}^{\left(1\right)}

\displaystyle=\mp\left.\tau\left[\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right|_{\pm a}^{t}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau ik^{2}\left[\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left[\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left[\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\psi\left(k\right)\frac{i\tau k^{2}}{\left(1+i\tau k^{2}\right)^{2}}\right)\right]\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau.

Observe that

\left\|\left(\tau k^{2}\right)^{l}\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\left(\frac{\tau k^{2}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)^{m}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|},

for

Then, by (

), (

), and (

) we
show

\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(1\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\pm t\geq a.

(4.83)

Next
,
using that

e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=\partial_{\tau}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}-ik^{2}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}

we write

as

\tilde{A}^{\left(2\right)}=\tilde{A}^{\left(21\right)}+\tilde{A}^{\left(22\right)}+\tilde{A}^{\left(23\right)},

with

\tilde{A}^{\left(21\right)}=-2\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\partial_{\tau}\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau,

\tilde{A}^{\left(22\right)}=4i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau k\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau

and

\tilde{A}^{\left(23\right)}=2i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau k^{2}\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}d\tau.

Integrating by parts in
\tau
we have

\displaystyle\tilde{A}^{\left(21\right)}

\displaystyle=\mp 2\left.\tau\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right|_{\pm a}^{t}

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left[\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left[\partial_{k}\left(\psi\left(k\right)\frac{i\tau k^{2}}{\left(1+i\tau k^{2}\right)^{2}}\right)\right]\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau.

Observe that

\left\|\left\langle k\right\rangle k^{l}\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\left(\frac{\tau k^{2}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)^{m}\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\frac{l}{2}},

for

and

Then, using (
3.5
), (
3.23
),
(
3.7
), and (
A.257
) we estimate
\tilde{A}^{\left(2\right)}
as

\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(2\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(21\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(22\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(23\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\pm t\geq a.

(4.84)

We now estimate
\tilde{A}^{\left(3\right)}.
Let us define,

\displaystyle b_{1}

\displaystyle=-4i\mathcal{V}_{\ +}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(x\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+4\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ike^{i\tau k^{2}}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(0\right),

\displaystyle+8ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+4ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\partial_{x}^{2}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right),

\displaystyle b_{2}

\displaystyle=i\mathcal{V}_{+\ }\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\left(\partial_{x}\left(x^{2}\chi\right)\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-2ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\left(\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr,\pm}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),

b_{3}=-2i\tau\left(\partial_{\tau}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),

and

b_{4}=-4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\left(\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-2i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\left(\partial_{x}\chi\right)\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-2i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{\tau}\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

Using (
4.1
) in the last term in the right-hand side of (
4.80
) we have

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\varphi\right)

\displaystyle=-4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{itk^{2}}\varphi\left(0\right)+4\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}e^{itk^{2}}\left(\partial_{x}\varphi\right)\left(0\right)

\displaystyle+4\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}^{2}\varphi.

(4.85)

Then, by (
3.23
) and (
4.85
) we calculate

\partial_{k}^{2}\left(ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)x\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=b_{1}.

By (
4.1
) and (
3.23
) we have

\displaystyle-2i\tau\left(\partial_{\tau}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle=-i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)2i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle=-i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-i\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle=2i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)+2ik\mathcal{V}^{\left(+\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=-i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x^{2}\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+2ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\left(\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)\right).

Then

\displaystyle i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x^{2}\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=b_{3}-2ik\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(x\chi\left(\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)\right)+i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

Thus, it follows from (
4.85
) that

\partial_{k}^{2}\left(i\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\frac{x^{2}}{4}\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=b_{2}+b_{3}.

Moreover, since

\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\varphi\right)=-4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\varphi-2i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\partial_{x}\varphi\right),

we show,

\partial_{k}^{2}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=b_{4}.

Hence

\partial_{k}^{2}\left(e^{i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)=\sum_{j=1}^{4}b_{j}.

Therefore, we decompose
\tilde{A}^{\left(3\right)}
as

\tilde{A}^{\left(3\right)}=\tilde{A}^{\left(31\right)}+\tilde{A}^{\left(32\right)},

(4.86)

where

and

Using (
3.5
), (

), (
3.7
), (
4.79
), and
(
A.257
) we show,

(4.87)

In order to estimate

\displaystyle b_{3}+b_{4}

\displaystyle=-4\tau^{2}k\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\left(\chi\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle-2i\tau\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\left(\partial_{x}\chi\right)\partial_{\tau}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-2i\tau\partial_{\tau}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right).

Then, using (

as

\tilde{A}_{2}^{\left(32\right)}=4\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{3}\frac{k\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)\left(\chi\mathcal{A}_{2}^{\left(1\right)}\right)d\tau,

\tilde{A}_{3}^{\left(32\right)}=2i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{2}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(x\left(\partial_{x}\chi\right)\left(\mathcal{A}_{1}^{\left(1\right)}+\mathcal{A}_{2}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right)d\tau,

and

\tilde{A}_{4}^{\left(32\right)}=2i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\tau^{2}\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+\ }\left(\tau\right)\left(x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau.

Then, using(
3.31
), (

), (

), we

(4.89)

Using (
4.78
), it follows from (

), and (
A.257
),

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\tilde{A}_{2}^{\left(32\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(|\tau|^{3/2}\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2}^{\left(1\right)}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+|\tau|\left\|\mathcal{A}_{2}^{\left(1\right)}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\leq\\
\\
C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w\right),\pm t\geq a.\end{array}

\displaystyle-2i\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\psi\left(k\right)\left[\partial_{\tau}\left(\frac{\tau^{2}}{1+i\tau k^{2}}\right)\right]\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)x\chi\partial_{x}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau.

Then, using (

), (

), and (

(4.91)

Hence, by (

), (

), (

\left\|\tilde{A}^{\left(32\right)}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w\right),\pm t\geq a.

(4.92)

From (

), (

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\tilde{A}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w\right),\pm t\geq a.

Proof of Lemma
4.2
.
We begin by decomposing

\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}=B_{1}+B_{2},

(4.95)

with

B_{1}=\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),

and

B_{2}=\widehat{\mathcal{V}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right),

where we recall that
\chi
is defined by (
4.73
). Using (
4.1
) and
integrating by parts we have

\displaystyle\partial_{k}B_{1}

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}

\displaystyle-2S\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+2\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

(4.96)

Then, using (
3.5
), (
3.7
), (
A.18
),
(
A.62
), (
A.257
), we get

\left\|\partial_{k}B_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.97)

Next, we calculate

\partial_{k}B_{2}=\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+S\left(k\right)\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

(4.98)

Recall that
P_{\pm}
are the projectors on the eigenspaces of
S(0)

corresponding to the eigenvalues
\pm 1,
respectively (see Appendix

A
). Using (
3.23
) we decompose

\displaystyle\partial_{k}B_{2}

\displaystyle=\partial_{k}\left(\left(S\left(k\right)-S\left(0\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-2P_{+}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle+P_{-}\left(\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right).

(4.99)

We use Lemma
4.7
with
\psi\left(k\right)=S\left(k\right)-S\left(0\right)
to estimate the first term on the right-hand side of the
above equality. By (
A.61
), (
A.62
), and
(
A.63
)
\psi
satisfies (
4.74
)
.
Then, it follows
from (
4.75
) that

\begin{array}[c]{l}\left\|\partial_{k}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left(S\left(k\right)-S\left(0\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\end{array}

(4.100)

for
\pm t\geq a.

Moreover, by (
3.7
), and (
A.257
),

\left\|\mathcal{V}_{\pm}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\left\|\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.101)

Then,

\left\|2P_{+}\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)-\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a.

(4.102)

Let us assume that
w_{\pm}(k)=S(k)w_{\pm}(-k),k\in{\mathbb{R}},
or
equivalently,

w_{\pm}\left(-k\right)=S\left(-k\right)w_{\pm}\left(k\right),\qquad k\in{\mathbb{R}}.

(4.103)

Using (
4.103
) we have

\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(0\right)=\sqrt{\frac{i\tau}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\left(S\left(-k\right)+I\right)w_{\pm}\left(k\right)dk.

(4.104)

Let
\psi
satisfy (
4.74
). Since
e^{-i\tau k^{2}}=\left(1-2i\tau k^{2}\right)^{-1}\partial_{k}\left(ke^{-i\tau k^{2}}\right)
,
integrating by parts we show

\displaystyle\left|\sqrt{\frac{i\tau}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\psi\left(k\right)w_{\pm}\left(k\right)dk\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|k\partial_{k}\left(\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1-2itk^{2}}w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right)\right|dk

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{k\partial_{k}\psi\left(k\right)}{1-2i\tau k^{2}}w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right|dk+C\sqrt{|\tau|}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1-2i\tau k^{2}}w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right|dk

\displaystyle+C\sqrt{|\tau|}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|k\frac{\psi\left(k\right)}{1-2i\tau k^{2}}\partial_{k}w_{\pm}\left(k\right)\right|dk.

(4.105)

Hence

\left|\sqrt{\frac{i\tau}{2\pi}}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-i\tau k^{2}}\psi\left(k\right)w_{\pm}\left(k\right)dk\right|\leq C|\tau|^{-\rho/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})},

(4.106)

for any
0\leq\rho<1.
By (
A.61
), (
A.62
), and
(
A.63
),
\psi=P_{-}\left(S\left(-k\right)+I\right)
satisfies (
4.74
). Then, using (
4.106
) we show

\left|P_{-}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(0\right)\right|\leq C|\tau|^{-\rho/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}.

(4.107)

Since
\mathcal{N}
commutes with
P_{-},
using this estimate together with
(
A.262
), we get

\left|P_{-}f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(0\right)\right|\leq C|\tau|^{-\rho/2-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.108)

for any
0\leq\rho<1.
Further, by (
3.23
)

\displaystyle\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)=4\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\left(0\right)

\displaystyle+2\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)+2\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right).

(4.109)

Then, it follows from (
4.101
) and (
4.108
) that

\left\|P_{-}\left[\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)-\partial_{k}\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right]\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{\frac{1-\rho}{2}-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.110)

0\leq\rho<1.
Hence, using (
4.100
), (
4.102
) and (
4.110
) in
(
4.99
), and taking
\rho
sufficiently close to
1,
since
\alpha>2
we
obtain

\left\|\partial_{k}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}B_{2}\,d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}+1\right)\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.111)

Combining estimates (
4.97
) and (
4.111
) in (
4.95
) we attain
(
4.12
).

Proof of Lemma
4.3
.
Derivating (
4.96
), using (
4.1
) and
integrating by parts we have

\displaystyle\partial_{k}^{2}B_{1}

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{k}^{2}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}

\displaystyle-4\left(\partial_{k}S\left(k\right)\right)\mathcal{V}{{}_{+}}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle+4S\left(k\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}^{2}\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+4\mathcal{V}{\ }_{-}\left(\tau\right)\partial_{x}^{2}\left(\left(1-\chi\right)f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right).

Using (
3.7
), (
A.18
), (
A.62
),
(
A.63
), and (
A.257
), we get

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}B_{1}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|\tau|}\,|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\,\Lambda_{\pm}\left(\tau;w_{\pm}\right).

(4.112)

To estimate
\partial_{k}^{2}B_{2}
we decompose
B_{2}
as

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=\left(S\left(k\right)-S\left(0\right)\right)\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle+P_{+}\left(1-\chi\right)\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+P_{+}\chi\left(\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)

(4.113)

\displaystyle+P_{-}\left(-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)+\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right).

(4.114)

From now on we take
\chi
even. Then, using (
4.103
) we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(-x\right)dx

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}e^{i\tau\left(k+\frac{x}{2}\right)^{2}}\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}dx+\mathcal{\widetilde{W}}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi a_{1}\right),

with

e^{i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}=\frac{\partial_{\tau}\left(\tau e^{i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\right)}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)},

and integrating by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\chi\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{i\tau k^{2}}f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}(\chi w_{\pm})P_{-}\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{\cdot}{k}\right)w\right)\right)\left(0\right)d\tau

\displaystyle=\pm\chi\frac{2i}{\pi}\left.kf_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(\chi w_{\pm}\right)e^{i\tau k^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\tau^{3}e^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}w_{\pm}\left(k_{1}\right)dk_{1}\right|_{\pm a}^{t}

\displaystyle-\chi\frac{2i}{\pi}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}ke^{i\tau k^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\tau e^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}\partial_{\tau}\left(\frac{\tau^{2}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(\chi w_{\pm}\right)P_{-}w_{\pm}\left(k_{1}\right)\right)\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)dk_{1}d\tau.

(4.138)

First, using (
4.103
) we have

\displaystyle kf_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}(\chi w_{\pm})e^{i\tau k^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\tau^{3}e^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}w_{\pm}\left(k_{1}\right)dk_{1}

\displaystyle=kf_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}(\chi w_{\pm})e^{i\tau k^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\tau^{3}e^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}\left(S\left(-k_{1}\right)+I\right)w_{\pm}\left(k_{1}\right)dk_{1}.

(4.139)

Let
g\in W^{1,\infty}.
Similarly to (
4.105
), by using that

\left|\frac{\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\right|\leq C\left|\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)\right|\min\left\{\frac{1}{1+\tau k^{2}},\frac{1}{1+\tau k_{1}^{2}}\right\},

and (
A.18
), (
A.61
), (
A.62
), we estimate

\displaystyle K:=\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{ke^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}\left(S\left(-k_{1}\right)+I\right)g\left(k_{1}\right)dk_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}kk_{1}\partial_{k_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\frac{1}{1-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}\left(S\left(-k_{1}\right)+I\right)g\left(k_{1}\right)\right)dk_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\left|k\right|}{1+\tau k^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{1+\tau k_{1}^{2}}\left|P_{-}\left(S\left(-k_{1}\right)+I\right)g\left(k_{1}\right)\right|dk_{1}

(4.140)

\displaystyle+C\frac{\left|k\right|}{1+\tau k^{2}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{k_{1}}{1+\tau k_{1}^{2}}\left|S^{\prime}\left(-k_{1}\right)g\left(k_{1}\right)\right|dk_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\tau}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{1}{1+\tau k_{1}^{2}}\left|P_{-}\left(S\left(-k_{1}\right)+I\right)g^{\prime}\left(k_{1}\right)\right|dk_{1}.

Then, by (
4.4
),

K\leq\frac{C}{|\tau|^{\rho}}\frac{\left|k\right|}{1+\tau k^{2}}\left\|g\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}+\frac{C}{\tau^{1+\rho}}\left\|g^{\prime}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})},

(4.141)

0\leq\rho<1.
Using (
4.141
), from (
4.139
) via (
4.136
), we
get

\displaystyle\left\|\chi\frac{2i}{\pi}\left.kf_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}e^{i\tau k^{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{\tau^{3}e^{-i\tau k_{1}^{2}}}{1+i\tau\left(k^{2}-k_{1}^{2}\right)}\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{k_{1}}{k}\right)P_{-}w_{\pm}\left(k_{1}\right)dk_{1}\right|_{\pm a}^{t}\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+|\tau|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha},\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.142)

From the equation (
2.83
) for
w_{\pm},
using (
1.13
),
(
3.23
) , (
A.18
), (
A.30
), (
A.31
), (
A.32
),
(
A.62
), (
A.151
), and (
A.152
), we have

\left\|\partial_{\tau}w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},

(4.143)

and

\left\|\partial_{k}\partial_{\tau}w_{\pm}\right\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}9}\leq C|\tau|^{-\frac{\alpha}{2}+\frac{1}{2}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+|\tau|^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right).

(4.144)

Therefore, arguing as in the proof of (
4.142
), by using (
4.135
),
(
4.4
), (
4.143
) and (
4.144
) we control the second term on
the right-hand side of (

) by

C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}+|\tau|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right),\pm t\geq a.

Together with (
4.142
) this proves that

\displaystyle\left\|\chi P_{-}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{i\tau k^{2}}f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}(\chi w_{\pm})\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(1-\zeta\right)\left(\frac{\cdot}{k}\right)w_{\pm}\right)\right)\left(0\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}+|\tau|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right).

(4.145)

Gathering (
4.137
) and (
4.145
) in (
4.132
) we arrive to

\displaystyle\left\|\chi P_{-}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{i\tau k^{2}}f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(\chi w_{\pm}\right)\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(0\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1}+|\tau|^{-1/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\right)\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right)=

(4.146)

\displaystyle C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right)\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

By (
1.13
) and (
A.152
)

\left|f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(w\right)-f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(\chi w\right)\right|\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left|\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)\left(\left(1-\chi\right)w\right)\right|\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}}^{\alpha-1}.

Then, as
1-\chi
satisfies (
4.74
), by (
4.106
), and, as
P_{-}

conmutes with
\mathcal{N},
(

), and (

), we get,

\left\|\chi P_{-}4i\tau\sqrt{\frac{\tau}{2\pi i}}ke^{i\tau k^{2}}\left(f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(w_{\pm}\right)-f_{2}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\left(\chi w_{\pm}\right)\right)\left(\mathcal{V}\left(\tau\right)w_{\pm}\right)\left(0\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{\frac{3}{2}-\rho-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.147)

0\leq\rho<1.
Finally, by using (
3.7
), and (
A.257
) we
show

\left\|\mathcal{V}^{\left(\pm\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(\partial_{x}^{2}\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}.

(4.148)

Hence, by (
4.4
), (
4.4
), (
4.147
), (
4.148
), and
since
\alpha>2,
we prove that

\displaystyle\left\|\chi P_{-}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\partial_{k}^{2}\left(-\mathcal{V}_{+}\left(\tau\right)+\mathcal{V}_{-}\left(\tau\right)\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right)\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.149)

Using (
3.7
), and(
A.253
), we show

\left\|\left(\partial_{k}^{j}\chi\right)\mathcal{V}^{\left(\pm\right)}\left(\tau\right)\left(\chi f_{1,\pm}^{\operatorname*{fr}}\right)\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C|\tau|^{-\alpha/2}\left\|w\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha+1},\qquad\pm\tau\geq a,

(4.150)

for
j=0,1,2.
Therefore, using (
4.110
), (
4.149
) and (
4.150
) in
(
4.130
) we prove

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\int_{a}^{t}B_{2}^{\left(2\right)}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right)\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right),\qquad\pm t\geq a.

(4.151)

Then, using (
4.115
), (

), and (

), we get

\left\|\partial_{k}^{2}\int_{\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}B_{2}d\tau\right\|_{L^{2}({\mathbb{R}})}\leq C\sqrt{|t|}\sup_{\tau\in\mathcal{T}_{\pm}(a,t)}\left(\Lambda_{\pm}(\tau;w_{\pm})+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{2\alpha+1}\right)\left(1+\left\|w_{\pm}\right\|_{H^{1}({\mathbb{R}})}^{\alpha}\right),

(4.152)

for
\pm t\geq a.
Finally, using (

) and (

) in

), we attain (
